            \HyperRaiseLinkHook
        \expandafter\endgroup
        \expandafter\raise\the\@tempdima\hbox{ 
            \Hy@RestoreSpaceFactor
            \hypertarget{proof:prop:basis_bound}{} 
            \Hy@SaveSpaceFactor
        } 
        }} 
        \Hy@RestoreSpaceFactor
        \penalty\@M\hskip\z@
    \fi
F or the ordinary lower bound, let $n\le(2/\alpha)^p$ and consider the fixed functionals $g_1,\ldots,g_n$ with zero thresholds.
For every $(\varepsilon_1,\ldots,\varepsilon_n)\in\{\pm1\}^n$, let $ u
    =
    \frac{\alpha}{2}
    \sum_{j=1}^n
    \varepsilon_j\phi_j$.
Since $n\le(2/\alpha)^p$, 
$\|u\|^p
    =
    n\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)^p
    \le1$,  
so $u\in 
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:feasible-set}{\normalcolor \mathcal X\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
$. Moreover, for each $j\le n$, $\varepsilon_j\langle g_j,u\rangle
    =
    \frac{\alpha}{2}$.
Thus $g_1,\ldots,g_n$ are $\alpha$-fat-shattered with zero thresholds, and $ 
     
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:fat-shattering-dimension}{\normalcolor \operatorname{fat}\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
[#1][#2] 
[\alpha]( 
     
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:linear-pairing-class}{\normalcolor \mathcal L\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
[#1][#2] 
[ 
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:feasible-set}{\normalcolor \mathcal X\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
, 
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:subgradient-set}{\normalcolor \mathcal H\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
])
    \gtrsim_p
    \alpha^{-p}$. \medskip



For the sequential upper bound, consider an $\alpha$-shattered tree of depth $n$. We construct one root-to-leaf path $\varepsilon=(\varepsilon_1,\dots,\varepsilon_n)$ greedily.
Fix a coordinate $g_j$. We prove by induction that, after its $k$-th occurrence along the constructed path, we have $|\langle g_j,u\rangle|
    \ge
    \left(k-\frac12\right)\alpha$ 
for every $u\in 
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:feasible-set}{\normalcolor \mathcal X\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
$ realizing the current partial path, i.e., satisfying all the shattering inequalities corresponding to the branches chosen so far (there is at least one because the tree is $\alpha$-shattered). For $k=1$, choose the branch whose constraint lies away from zero, which gives $|\langle g_j,u\rangle|\ge\alpha/2$.
For the induction step, suppose for instance that after the $k$th occurrence we have $\langle g_j,u\rangle\ge(k-\frac12)\alpha$. At the next occurrence, let $r$ be the threshold. Since the tree is shattered, the negative child is realizable, so there exists some $u^-\in 
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:feasible-set}{\normalcolor \mathcal X\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
$ satisfying the previous constraints together with
$\langle g_j,u^-\rangle\le r-\alpha/2$. By the induction hypothesis, the same $u^-$ satisfies
$\langle g_j,u^-\rangle\ge(k-\frac12)\alpha$. Hence
\[
    r-\frac{\alpha}{2}
    \ge
    \langle g_j,u^-\rangle
    \ge
    \left(k-\frac12\right)\alpha,
\]
so $r\ge k\alpha$. Choosing the positive child then gives
$\langle g_j,u\rangle\ge r+\alpha/2\ge(k+\frac12)\alpha$. The negative case is symmetric.




Thus, if $k_j$ denotes the number of occurrences of $g_j$ along the resulting path, any witness $u\in 
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:feasible-set}{\normalcolor \mathcal X\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
$ for that path satisfies
$|\langle g_j,u\rangle|\ge(k_j-\frac12)\alpha$ whenever $k_j>0$. Since $\sum_j k_j=n$,
\begin{align*}
    1
    \ge \|u\|^p=
    \sum_j |\langle g_j,u\rangle|^p\ge
    \alpha^p\sum_{j:k_j>0}\left(k_j-\frac12\right)^p\ge
    \left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)^p\sum_j k_j
    =
    \left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)^p n.
\end{align*}
Hence $n\le(2/\alpha)^p$, so
$ 
     
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:sequential-fat-shattering-dimension}{\normalcolor \operatorname{sfat}\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
[#1][#2] 
[\alpha]( 
     
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:linear-pairing-class}{\normalcolor \mathcal L\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
[#1][#2] 
[ 
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:feasible-set}{\normalcolor \mathcal X\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
, 
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:subgradient-set}{\normalcolor \mathcal H\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
])\lesssim_p\alpha^{-p}$.
For the optimization consequence, replace $ 
    {\protect\hyperlink{def:subgradient-set}{\normalcolor \mathcal H\IfBlankF{#3}{_{#3}}\IfBlankF{#4}{^{#4}}}} 
$ by $\{0\}\cup\{\pm g_j:j\ge1\}$. This does not change either shattering dimension.
<!-- end proof-28 -->
